This is a perception-only benchmark workspace. Use this file as the scoped
replacement for the full DexHoldem skill.

- `s_current/00_capture.jpg`: the current table image.
- `s_current` symlink target and prior `sN/` folders when temporal comparison
  is needed.
- `hole_card_cache.json` and `action_sequence.json` for cached state context.
- `visual_guidelines/` for table geometry and visual parsing rules.
- visible visual subagents under `.codex/agents` or `.claude/agents`.

Main-agent task:
1. Launch the needed visual subagents for image-reading questions.
2. Merge only returned subagent evidence.
3. Write one raw evidence file per called subagent under
   `runs/<run_id>/visual_raw/`.
4. Write `runs/<run_id>/visual_summary.json`.
5. Write `runs/<run_id>/eval_report.md`.
6. Verify the required files exist and stop.

Do not run helper scripts. Do not execute robot actions. Do not create or
advance state folders. Do not call a reasoning agent. Do not choose a poker
action.
